The project uses old versions of\n`compile-testing` library which have a hard dependency on `com.sun:tools` (tools.jar).\nThis file does not exist in Java 9+ as the Java compiler classes were modularized.\n\n## Environment\n- Java Version: OpenJDK 11.0.27\n- Build Tool: Maven\n\n## Root Cause\nThe `compile-testing` library in versions 0.6, 0.7, and 0.8 declares `com.sun:tools` as\na direct dependency with system scope pointing to `${toolsjar}` (tools.jar):\n\n```xml\n<dependency>\n  <groupId>com.sun</groupId>\n  <artifactId>tools</artifactId>\n  <version>${java.version}</version>\n  <scope>system</scope>\n  <systemPath>${toolsjar}</systemPath>\n</dependency>\n```\n\nIn Java 8 and earlier, tools.jar contained the Java compiler API classes.\nIn Java 9+, these classes are part of the java.compiler module and tools.jar doesn't exist.\n\nStarting from compile-testing version 0.19, this dependency was moved to a Maven profile\nthat only activates when tools.jar exists, allowing the library to work with Java 9+.\n\n## Affected Modules and Required Changes\n\n### 1. common/pom.xml\n- Current: compile-testing version 0.7\n- Fix: Upgrade to version 0.19\n\n### 2. service/pom.xml\n- Current: compile-testing version 0.6\n- Fix: Upgrade to version 0.19\n\n### 3. factory/pom.xml\n- Current: compile-testing version 0.8\n- Fix: Upgrade to version 0.19\n\n### 4. value/pom.xml\n- Current: compile-testing version 0.6\n- Fix: Upgrade to version 0.19\n\n## Error Messages\nAll modules fail with:\n```\nCould not find artifact com.sun:tools:jar:11.0.27 at specified path\n/usr/lib/jvm/java-11-openjdk-amd64/../lib/tools.jar\n```\n\n## Solution\nUpgrade compile-testing dependency from old versions (0.6, 0.7, 0.8) to version 0.19\nin all four module pom.xml files. The project uses old versions of\n`compile-testing` library which have a hard dependency on `com.sun:tools` (tools.jar).\nThis file does not exist in Java 9+ as the Java compiler classes were modularized.\n\n## Environment\n- Java Version: OpenJDK 11.0.27\n- Build Tool: Maven\n\n## Root Cause\nThe `compile-testing` library in versions 0.6, 0.7, and 0.8 declares `com.sun:tools` as\na direct dependency with system scope pointing to `${toolsjar}` (tools.jar):\n\n```xml\n<dependency>\n  <groupId>com.sun</groupId>\n  <artifactId>tools</artifactId>\n  <version>${java.version}</version>\n  <scope>system</scope>\n  <systemPath>${toolsjar}</systemPath>\n</dependency>\n```\n\nIn Java 8 and earlier, tools.jar contained the Java compiler API classes.\nIn Java 9+, these classes are part of the java.compiler module and tools.jar doesn't exist.\n\nStarting from compile-testing version 0.19, this dependency was moved to a Maven profile\nthat only activates when tools.jar exists, allowing the library to work with Java 9+.\n\n## Affected Modules and Required Changes\n\n### 1. common/pom.xml\n- Current: compile-testing version 0.7\n- Fix: Upgrade to version 0.19\n\n### 2. service/pom.xml\n- Current: compile-testing version 0.6\n- Fix: Upgrade to version 0.19\n\n### 3. factory/pom.xml\n- Current: compile-testing version 0.8\n- Fix: Upgrade to version 0.19\n\n### 4. value/pom.xml\n- Current: compile-testing version 0.6\n- Fix: Upgrade to version 0.19\n\n## Error Messages\nAll modules fail with:\n```\nCould not find artifact com.sun:tools:jar:11.0.27 at specified path\n/usr/lib/jvm/java-11-openjdk-amd64/../lib/tools.jar\n```\n\n## Solution\nUpgrade compile-testing dependency from old versions (0.6, 0.7, 0.8) to version 0.19\nin all four module pom.xml files. Version 0.19 uses a profile-based approach that\nonly adds the tools.jar dependency when running on Java 8 or earlier.\n\n## Additional Issue: javax.annotation.Generated\n\n### 5. factory/pom.xml - Missing javax.annotation.Generated\nIn Java 9+, `javax.annotation.Generated` was removed from the standard library.\nThe code uses this annotation but doesn't have the dependency.\nFix: Add javax.annotation-api dependency.\n\n### 6. value/pom.xml - Missing auto-common dependency\nThe auto-value module depends on `com.google.auto:auto-common:1.0-SNAPSHOT` which\ndoesn't exist in remote repositories. It needs to be built locally first, or the\nversion should be changed to a published release (0.5).\nFix: Change auto-common version from 1.0-SNAPSHOT to 0.5.\n\n## Patches Required\n1. patch_1.diff - common/pom.xml: compile-testing 0.7 -> 0.19\n2. patch_2.diff - service/pom.xml: compile-testing 0.6 -> 0.19\n3. patch_3.diff - factory/pom.xml: compile-testing 0.8 -> 0.19\n4. patch_4.diff - value/pom.xml: compile-testing 0.6 -> 0.19\n5. patch_5.diff - factory/pom.xml: Add javax.annotation-api dependency\n6. patch_6.diff - value/pom.xml: auto-common 1.0-SNAPSHOT -> 0.5\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":72,"oldLines":6,"newStart":72,"newLines":11,"lines":[" version should be changed to a published release (0.5)."," Fix: Change auto-common version from 1.0-SNAPSHOT to 0.5."," ","+### 7. value/pom.xml - Missing javax.annotation.Generated","+Same issue as factory module - the code uses javax.annotation.Generated which","+was removed from the standard library in Java 9+.","+Fix: Add javax.annotation-api dependency.","+"," ## Patches Required"," 1. patch_1.diff - common/pom.xml: compile-testing 0.7 -> 0.19"," 2. patch_2.diff - service/pom.xml: compile-testing 0.6 -> 0.19"]},{"oldStart":79,"oldLines":3,"newStart":84,"newLines":4,"lines":[" 4. patch_4.diff - value/pom.xml: compile-testing 0.6 -> 0.19"," 5. patch_5.diff - factory/pom.xml: Add javax.annotation-api dependency"," 6. patch_6.diff - value/pom.xml: auto-common 1.0-SNAPSHOT -> 0.5","+7. patch_7.diff - value/pom.xml: Add javax.annotation-api dependency"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
